mītum poṟṟaviciṭṭu iṭiyaṟātu nirittuccellum kaṭalpōṉṟa cēṉi apayaṉ immuṟi cētupantaṉañ ceytatottatu. nīlamaṇikaḷ nāṟṟiya civiki veḷḷamum muttuk kuṭi veḷḷamum, kālvāykaḷāṟ perukum yamuṉi veḷḷatti ottaṉa. mīṉ, pāmpu, kaluḻaṉ, yāṉi, paṉṟi, āḷi, kutiri, mēḻi, kōḻi, vil mutaliya āyiraṅ koṭikaḷ acintaṉa. kaṭiya ciṉappulik koṭikaḷ avaṟṟiṉ mēlāka viḷaṅkiṉa. māli aṇinta kaḻuttukaḷum, pūccūṭiṉa kūntalum, mirutu vāṉa naṭiyum, iṉiya moḻiyum, eṉṟum oppillāta nakiyumuṭiya makaḷir kūṭṭaṅkaḷ evviṭattum parantu ceṉṟaṉa. ap peṇkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, 1melliya katalikaḷum, paccik kamukum, poṅkum iḷanīrk kulikaḷum āṟṟiṭattu eḻuñ cuḻikaḷum, aṉṉanaṭiyum, āli yiṭattuk kamaḻkiṉṟa pākum evviṭattum parantu, vēṟumoru poṉṉi vaḷa nāṭu cayatuṅkaṉmuṉ niṉṟatu pōṉṟatu. eṅkum neruṅkic cellum vēḻa nirikaḷākiya malikaḷōṭu cellum veṟṟivēl apayaṉ, taṉatu aruḷippeṟṟu vāḻtalāl apayam pukunta cēraṉōṭu kūṭa malināṭu kāṇacceṉṟavaṉ pōṉṟāṉ. avvāṉikaḷākiya malikkūṭṭaṅkaḷ tutikkikaḷiṉāṟ ṟeṟikkum nuṇṇiya nīrttivali, pāṇṭiyar at tikkil añci viṭṭōṭiya mutti vārikkoṇṭu teṉṟaṟkāṟṟu varuvati ottatu.
ivvaki āṭamparaṅkaḷōṭu puṟappaṭṭa vicayataraṉ teṉticiyi ṉiṉṟum vaṭakku mukamākat tirumpic citamparattukkuc ceṉṟu civaperu māṉit taricittu, avararuḷip peṟṟukkoṇṭu atikimānakari aṭintāṉ. piṉ atikiyiṉiṉṟum payaṇappaṭṭu vēṭṭiyāṭi vaṭṭamati otta kuṭiyuṭiya maṉṉar toḻa vaḷam poruntiya kaccinakar aṭintāṉ.
ivvāṟu itantarum varalāṟṟi iṟivi collik koṇṭiruk kumpōtu pōrkkaḷattiṉiṉṟum oru pēy kālpiṟpaṭa maṉattu uvaki taḷḷivara ōṭi vantatu. vantu kaliṅkaratu irattam veḷḷam, veḷḷamā yōṭukiṉṟatu. kaliṅkatēcattukku, avacaram, avacaramākac celluṅkaḷ; ceṉṟu melinta uṭali nirappuṅkaḷ! nirappuṅkaḷ!! tēviyiṉ kaṇaṅkaḷē! ēṉ iṅkirukkiṉṟīrkaḷ. itaṟkumuṉṉē valiya ciṟakuṭiya kaḻutiṉatum paruttiṉatum vayiṟukaḷ pīṟippōyiṉa; aṅkuḷḷa piṇaṅkaḷi uṇpa taṟku uṅkaḷ vayiṟukaḷum pōtā; eṉṟālum ellōrum tiraṇṭu ōṭic celluṅkaḷ; ciramalikaḷi mēlum mēlum viḻuṅkavum, tiri eḻukiṉṟa irattak kaṭalik kuṭikkavum piramaṉi vēṇṭi iṉṉum perum paciyip peṟuṅkaḷ” eṉṟatu.
ivvāṟu kūṟak kēṭṭa pēykaḷ, koḻutta piṇam tiṉṟaṉapōl uṭal pūrittuc cirittu oṉṟiṉmēl oṉṟu viḻuntaṉa. makiḻtaṟkuriya kaliṅkappōri aṟivitta pēyiṉ vāyi ōṭi ōṭi muttamiṭṭaṉa. cāvēm eṉṟa pēykaḷiṉ paṟkaḷit takaruṅkaḷ eṉṟaṉa. okkalip piḷḷi viḻaviḻap peruntuṇaṅki āṭiṉa; vaḷḷip pāṭṭuppāṭi āṭac cila pēykaḷi aḻittaṉa; kaṉāvuritta pēyik kaḻuttiṉiṟ koṇṭu, arippaṭṭi pūṇṭa nantēvi vāḻka! vāḻka!! eṉṟu āṭiṉa. ivvāṟu tuḷḷik kutittu āṭal puriyum pēykaḷiṉ āṭalit tēvi tavirttāḷ; tavirttup pōrkkaḷattiṉiṉṟum vanta pēyi nōkki, “appōr nikaḻnta vakiyik kūṟu” eṉa appēy kūṟukiṉṟatu.

1kāḷikkuk kūḷikūṟiyatu
tēvī! yāṉikaḷ āyiram paṭak kaliṅkar maṭinta kaḷappōr urip pōrkku nāvāyiramum kēṭpōrkku nāḷāyiramum vēṇṭum; itaṉi, oruvarkku oruvar oru nāviṉāṟ kūṟa muṭiyāteṉiṉum ciṟiyēṉ viṇṇappañ ceykiṉṟēṉ; kēṭṭaruḷ. muṉṉoru nāḷ, pārellām uṭiya apayaṉatu centāmari malarotta koṭikki nikareṉa mēkam iruṇṭu kāñci nakariṭattu ēḻari nāḻiki poṉmaḻi peytatu. an nakariṭattu, aḻakiya poṉmayamāṉa mēru ituvō aṉṟi atuvō eṉṟu, valam varuvataṟkuc cūriyaṉ cantēka muṟukiṉṟa irāca māḷikiyiṉ teṉmēṟku mūliyil cittira maṇṭapamoṉṟuṇṭu. ataṉ naṭuvē, moyttu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa tārakikaḷukku naṭuvileḻunta pūraṇa cantiraṉukku oppeṉa, aḻakupeṟṟu viḷaṅkiya muttup pantariṉ naṭuvē cantiravaṭṭak kuṭi oṉṟu niḻaṟceyyā niṉṟatu. ataṉkīḻ vīcukiṉṟa veṇcāmarikaḷ, tiruppāṟkaṭaliṉ iraṇṭu tirikaḷ vantu paṇi ceyvatu pōṉṟaṉa; avaṟṟiṉ iṭiyē iṭaṉakaṉṟa ulakam muḻuvatiyum puyattil vittu mēruviṟ pulikkuṟi poṟitta vicayataraṉ ciṅkācaṉattiliruppatōr ciṅkameṉa viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. pāmpiṉ paṭattiṟṟaṅkuṉṟa pūmikku nāyakaṉum, nāviṟ pala kalikaḷ uṟiyap peṟṟavaṉumākiya vicayataraṉatu maṇika ḷaḻuttiya paṇikaḷ pūṇṭa puyattē ciṅkavākaṉi vantu ilakkumiyōṭu amarntāḷ. kaṟpakataru nāṇumpaṭi poṉṉip piṟarkkut tāṉamākap poḻikiṉṟaṉa ākiya taṉ puyattiviṭṭu nīṅkāta vīramātum tirumātum pōlap perum puṇṇiyañ ceyta tiyākavalliyum ēḻici vallapiyum ākiya tēviyar cēvittiruntaṉar. nāṭakāti niruttam aṉittum 2nāl vakip perumpaṇ eṉṟu collappaṭṭa āṭal pāṭalkaḷiṟ ciṟanta arampayari otta peṇkaḷanēkar pakkattē niṉṟaṉar. niṉṟēttuvārum, iruntēttuvārum, tūyamaṅkalam pāṭuvārum ‘niṉpātattil vīḻntu vaṇaṅkiya aracarukkellām pacum poṉ muṭi’ eṉap pukaḻntu pāṭiṉar. vīṇi, yāḻ, kuḻal, mattaḷam mutaliyaṉa vallōr, “ivi vēṟu vēṟu nūṟu vitampaṭak kāṇalām vaki yām kaṟṟaṟintuḷḷēm ivaṟṟi nī kaṇ ṭaruḷalvēṇṭu” meṉak kūṟip pātaṅkaḷiṟ paṇintu niṉṟaṉar. tāḷamum iciyum piḻiyā vaki tāṉ vakutta pāṭalkaḷit taṉṉetiriṟ pāṭic ciṉṉamum kaḷiṟum peṟumpāṇariṉ kalviyiṟ piḻikaṇṭu 1kēṭṭirun tāṉ. aḻakiya yāṉiyiviṭṭu iḻintuvantu aṭiyil vīḻnta maṉṉar mutukukāṭṭic cellātu tamatu poṟkuṭi cāmari eṉṟivaṟṟit taṅ karattāṟ paṇimāṟiṉar. pāṇṭiyarāti aracapattiṉikaḷic cēṭiyarāka vuṭiya taṉ tēvimāruṭaṉ vicayataraṉ intiraṉippōṟ koluvirun tāṉ. kuṟunila maṉṉarum neṭunila vēntarum vantu vaṇaṅkum vāyi liṭattu vaṇṭi maṉṉaṉākiya toṇṭimāṉ mutaliya mantiri pārakar cūḻntu niṉṟaṉar. attaruṇattut 3tirumantira ōli nāyakaṉ aracaṉ muṉpōntu vaṇaṅki, “vēntarperuma! vēntar, tam tiṟiyic celuttu vataṟku vāyiṟpuṟattē niṟkiṉṟaṉar” eṉak kūṟi niṉṟu, nōkkiṉāl avaṉ kuṟippaṟintu avarkaḷi uḷḷē pukuttiṉāṉ. 
“teṉṉavar villavar kūvakar
cāvakar cētipar yātavarē
kaṉṉaṭar pallavar kitavar 
kāṭavar kāripar kōcalarē.”
“kaṅkar kaṭārar pavintar 
tumintar kaṭampar tuḷumparkaḷē
vaṅka rilāṭar marāṭar
virāṭar mayintar cayintarkaḷē.”
“ciṅkaḷar vaṅkaḷar cēkuṇar 
cēvaṇar cīyaṇa riyaṇarē
koṅkaṇar koṅkar kuliṅka
ravantiyar kuccarar kacciyarē.”
“vattavā mattirar māḷuvar
mākatar maccar milēccarkaḷē
kuttirar kuttar kuṭakkar
piṭakkar kurukkar turukkarkaḷē.”
eṉṉum pala tēcattu aracarkaḷellām, “ceyatuṅkaṉē! nī emakku aruḷ ceyta nāṭu nakarkaḷiṉ poruṭṭāka iṭappaṇitta tiṟikaḷi ellām coṉṉapaṭi tavaṟātu koṇarntaṉam: ēṟṟaruḷka” eṉap pukaṉṟu kūppiya karaṅkaḷōṭu pātattil vīḻntu vaṇaṅkiṉar; vaṇaṅki, 
“āramivi yivi poṟkala māṉi yiviyivi yoṭṭaka
māṭalayamivi maṟṟiviyāti muṭiyoṭu peṭṭaka
mīramuṭiyaṉa nittilamēṟu navamaṇi kaṭṭiya 
vēkavaṭamivi maṟṟivi yātum viliyil patakkamē.”
“iviyu miviyum maṇittiraḷiṉiya viṉiya taṉakkuvi 
iruḷum veyilu meṟittiṭa vilakumivi makarakkuḻi
yuviyu muviyu milakkaṇamuṭiya piṭiyuvi 1puṭpaka
muyarcey koṭiyivi maṟṟivi yurimi yariviyar paṭṭamē.”
eṉak kūṟit tāṅkoṇarnta kappaṅkaḷi ellām koṇṭu muṉ niṉṟaṉar. tiṟi celuttiya aracaruṭ cilar, “innūṟu karikaḷum ēṟiccelaṟku vantaṉa. ivviyāṉikaḷukku nikarāka vēṟu aracar ōr āṉi taruva reṉil ematu nāṭṭi avaruk kaḷikka icivōm” eṉat taṉittaṉi tam vāḻvu karutik kūṟi niṉṟaṉar. tiṟiceluttip paṇinta aracariṉ ciraṅkaḷ mītu vicayataraṉ taṉ irupātaṅkaḷiyum vittu “añcēl” eṉṟu apaya maḷittāṉ; aḷittu, “iṅku tiṟikoṭuttu niṉṟavarkaḷoḻiya tiṟi koṭukkat tavaṟiyavarkaḷumuṇṭō?” eṉat tirumantira ōli nāyakaṉi viṉāvalum, avaṉ “vēttar peruma! tattan tiṟikaḷuṭaṉ vantu ēṉi vēntarellām tāḷ niḻal vaṇaṅkiṉarāyiṉum, kaliṅka aracaṉākiya aṉanta vaṉmaṉ eṉpavaṉ maṭṭum uriya tiṟiyōṭum irumuṟiyāka vantilaṉ” eṉṟāṉ. ataṉik kēṭṭatum vicayataraṉ mukattiṟ puṉmuṟu val arumpiyatē yaṉṟic ciṟitum kōpak kuṟitōṉṟiyatilli. vantu niṉṟa ēṉi maṉṉarkaḷ, “iṉi nikaḻvatu yātō?” eṉa uṭal kulintu naṭunaṭuṅkalāyiṉar. appōtu, cōḻaṉ, taṉ paṭittalivari nōkkik “kaliṅkarpati paṭi valiyaṟṟatāyiṉum avaṉ kuṉṟaraṇ peruvalimi koṇṭatu. ataṉāl nam yāṉip paṭiyuṭaṉ nīvīr ceṉṟu kaliṅkaṉip piṭittu varutir” eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷi yiṭṭaṉaṉ. ivvāṟu aracaṉ moḻitalum vētaṅkaḷik kūṟiya piramāviṉ marapil vanta pallava kulatilakaṉum vaṇṭi nakarkkaracaṉum, vicayataraṉatu matimantiriyum, talimic cēṉāpati yumākiya karuṇākarattoṇṭimāṉ eḻuntaṭi vaṇaṅki, “vēntar vēntē! yāṉē paṭi eṭuttuc ceṉṟu ēḻu kaliṅkaṅkaḷiyum aḻittu varuvēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. iṅṅaṉam kūṟi virintu paki aḻikkum viruppuṭaṉ avaṉ aracaṉpāl viṭikēṭka avaṉum aṅṅaṉamē ceyka eṉa viṭi koṭuttaruḷiṉāṉ.
paṭiyeḻucci
kaṭalik kalakkuvatō, maliyi iṭippatō, kaṭiya viṭamum poṟiyumuṭiya āticēṭaṉiṉ piṭariyi muṟippatō, ippaṭiyiṉ niṉivu eṉumpaṭi nālvakip paṭiyum piraḷayam pōṟṟiraṇṭaṉa. vaḷi, muracu, vayir mutaliya palliyaṅkaḷ ārttaṉa; cāmarikaḷ iraṭṭiṉa; kuṭikaḷ nirittaṉa; mayiṟpīlikkuṭikaḷ neruṅkiṉa; koṭikaḷ parantaṉa. ivi orumittucceṉṟu niḻal ceytaliṉ pūmi kuḷirnta nāṟcantiyi ottatu. koṭi, kuṭi mutaliyaṉa nirittaliṉ cūriyaṉ maṟintu pūmiyil iruḷ kavintatu. eṇṇillāta paricikaḷ taḻaleḻa miṉṉutalālum ariya poṉṉāparaṇaṅkaḷ kaṉalpōl viḷaṅkutalālum, viḷaṅkukiṉṟa paṭik kalaṅkaḷ oḷiviṭutalālum, avviruḷ ōṭi maṟintatu; oḷi parantatu.
“akila veṟpumiṉṟāṉi yāṉavō
vaṭiya mārutam puraviyāṉavō
mukila ṉittumat tērka ḷāṉavō
mūrivēli pōr vīra rāṉavō.”
“pārci ṟuttaliṟ paṭi peruttatō
paṭipe ruttaliṟ pārci ṟuttatō
nērce ṟuttavark karitu niṟpiṭa
neṭuvi cumpalā liṭamu milliyē.”
eṉa maṇṇuḷḷōrum viṇṇuḷḷōrum eṭuttu moḻintu ati cayittu maṉam naṭukkuṟa vāṉmaṟitta nāṟṟicikaḷ eṅkum nālvakip paṭikaḷum tiraṇṭu parantu niṉṟaṉa. kaṭalkaḷic coriyum maliyuḷa veṉumpaṭi yāṉikaḷ 1cuvaṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum matam poḻintaṉa; neruppi umiḻum mukilkaḷ uḷaveṉa viḻikaḷiṟ ciṉakkaṉal cintiṉa; yāṉi kaḷōṭu parikaḷiṉ uṭaliyum piḷakkum cila piṟikaḷ uṇṭeṉumpaṭi uyarnta maruppōṭu viḷaṅkiṉa. ulaki nilikulikkumpaṭi muḻaṅkum vaṭavi uṇṭeṉa avi neruṅki muḻaṅkiṉa.
kutirikaḷ etirttavar muṭiyiṉi iṭaṟuva; muṭiyiṉ muttiṉip pukaḻiṉ viḷiveṉa nilattiṟ cintuva; nilattileḻum pūtūḷi aṟa mukili mitippaṉa; mukilviṭukiṉṟa tuḷiyōṭu, olikkiṉṟa kaṭaṟṟiriyeṉa viri voṭu kaṭal cūḻnta nilatti valamiṭamāka varuvaṉa; kaṭaliṭattu viḻum iṭiyeṉa aṭiyeṭuttu vikkum katiyiṉa. ivvāṟāṉa kutirip paṭikaḷ vēkattōṭu ceṉṟaṉa. irataṅkaḷ periya nilattiṉ tiṭarkaḷi uṭikkum uruḷiṉa; iru puṟattuñ ciṟakuṭiyaṉa; pōrmuṉiyil etirē paṟantu cellum celaviṉa; nukattiṟ pūṭṭiya kutirikaḷ vēkattōṭu muṉṉē cellum celaviṉa. atu tamakku avamāṉa meṉṉum 1oru niṉippiṉa. 2koṭiñcit tāmari malarpōl malarātiruttaṟkup pōrkkaḷattuḷḷa irattatti vāri iṟippaṉa; ulaki aḷappaṉa; ivviyalpiṉavākiya tērkaḷ parantu ceṉṟaṉa.
kālāṭkaḷ aḷaviṭavariya veṟṟiyum urimiyum iviyeṉap piṟar aṟiya avayavattileḻutiya aṟikuṟi avi eṉa, uṭampil pala āyutaṅkaḷ paṭṭa niritta taḻumpukaḷuṭiyar. tēvarulakōṭu ivvulakam kiṭippatāyiṉum piṉ aṭi eṭuttu viyāta kōṭpāṭṭiṉar; uṭal emakkoru cumi eṉṉum ciṉattiṉar. uyiri viṟṟup pukaḻkoḷḷa viḻipavar.
ivvāṟu oruvari otta pala vīrar neruṅkic ceṉṟaṉar. kaṇṇiṟ kōpattī eḻa viḻitta virutarkaḷ vekuṇṭu veṭicirippuc cirittu ataṭṭiyapoḻutu imayavar tiṭukkiṭṭaṉar; tikkiyāṉikaḷ tiṭukkiṭṭaṉa. ukamuṭivil eḻunta kaṭaliṉ perukku itu eṉumpaṭiyāṉa parikaḷ mukiliṉ muḻakkattilum mēlākak kaṉittu mukattiṉiṉṟum cintum nuri, kaṅkānatiyiṭattu eḻum nuripōṉṟatu.
kaṭṭuttaṟiyik kōpikkiṉṟa ciṉamum, periya kaṉṉattē corikiṉṟa matamum, nilattiṟ poṟutta aṭiyumuṭiyavāy nilattic cīyttu varukiṉṟa mukil pōṉṟa yāṉikaḷiṉ muḻakkil ali eḻukiṉṟa kaṭal nīrik kuṭitta mukilkaḷum piḷantaṉa. 
kaṭiya viciyuṭiya uruḷ toṭuttu ulaki oru kaṇattil valamvarum aḷaviṭaṟkariya tērkaḷiṉ mītu eṭutta koṭikaḷ tikku yāṉikaḷiṉ matamītirukkum vaṇṭukaḷi eḻuppiṉa.
ivvāṟu cēṉikaḷ eḻuntaṉa; eḻutalum pūmiyiṉ mutuku neḷintatu; kāṭukaḷ viḻuntaṉa; natikaḷ veṟuntariyākac cuvaṟi ularntaṉa; nāṉku ticikaḷum atirntaṉa; ēḻu kaṭalkaḷum aṭaṅkiṉa; periya malikaḷ poṭintaṉa. pūtūḷi eḻuntatu; nilattē eḻum tūḷiyip paruki vāṉiṉ vayiṟu niṟintatu; valamāka eḻukiṉṟa mukil nirikaḷ nīrcuvaṟi vaṟantaṉa; viḷaṅkukiṉṟa neṟṟippaṭṭa maṇinta malikaḷ coriyum mata veḷḷattiṉālum, kutirivāyiṉiṉṟu cintum nuriyiṉālum pūtūḷi aṭaṅkiṟṟu; ēkum ticiyi utayattē aṟintu, eḻukiṉṟa pūtūḷi aṭaṅ kumpaṭi naṭantu, mīḷac cūriyaṉ attakiriyil maṟiyumpōtu cellutal oḻintu, eppoḻutuñ ceṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu cellutaṟkut taṇṇiya malar māli yaṇinta upacēṉāpatikaḷōṭu, apayaṉatu paṭikaḷ ellāvaṟ ṟukkum kaṇpōṉṟavaṉum, cōḻaṉ cakkaramākiyavaṉumākiya karuṇākaraṉ yāṉi ivarntāṉ. toṇṭiyarkkaracaṉum kāmatēṉuviṉ vaḻiyil vanta paricuttamāṉa veḷḷiya iṭapakkoṭi uṭiyavaṉum, vaṇṭiyark karacaṉum, pallavarkkaracaṉumākiya karuṇākarat toṇṭimāṉ kaḷiṟṟiṉ mītu ēṟutalum, veṟṟikoṇṭu aracar yāṉiyik kavara vāṇarkō variyaṉ oru yāṉi mītēṟiṉāṉ pakivar tūcippaṭiyi vāḷāl veṉṟu muṭikoṇṭavaṉākiya muṭikoṇṭa cōḻaṉum oru mukapāṭam pōrtta kaḷiṟūrntāṉ.
evvaracarum puṟaṅkoṭuttōṭi maṭikaveṉṟu varum, varuttukiṉṟa pallavarkōṉum cōḻaṉum oḷiviṭṭu viḷaṅkum neṟṟippaṭṭa maṇinta kaḷiṟṟiṉmītu ēṟi iri vēṭṭa perumpulipōl ikalmēṟ ceṉṟaṉar. apperumpaṭi pālāṟu, kucittali, poṉmukari, paḻiya āṟu paṭarntu eḻukiṉṟa kolli āṟu, nāṉku āṟukaḷ cērntu akaṉṟa peṇṇi yāṟu mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṭantatu; vayalāṟu pukuntu maṇipōṉṟa nīrpāyum maṇṇāṟu. vaḷamporuntiya kuṉṟiyāṟu, parantu niṟintuvarum pērāṟu ākiyavaṟṟik kaṭantu, avi piṉṉē kaḻiyak kōtāvariyōṭu kuḷirnta nīr ōṭum vaḷamporuntiya kampi, cantanati, kōtami eṉṉum natikaḷit tāṇṭi, ukamuṭiviṟ perukivarukiṉṟa kaṭaliyottuk kaliṅka nāṭṭiṉuṭ pukuntatu. 
vīrar, taṅkaḷ varavi aṟivippavarpōla nāṭeṅkun tīkkoḷuvic cūṟiyāṭiṉar. kaṅkānati oru puṟamākak kiṭakkak kaṭalpōṟ paṭivantatu; “paṭivantāl eṅkē pukuvatu? araṇ iṉi eṅkē? iṉi ematu vitiyātō? eṉṟu kuṭikaḷ kalaṅkiṉa, matilkaḷ iṭikiṉṟaṉa; vīṭukaḷ erikiṉṟaṉa; poḻilkaḷellām puki eḻukiṉṟatu; maṭikiṉṟēm; paki eṉṟu paṭikaḷ vaḷikiṉṟaṉa” eṉṟu tuṭittaṉa. tuṭittu appaṭikaḷiṉ koṭumikku āṟṟāta makkaḷ ariyiṟ ṟukiluriyumpaṭi kaliṅkavēntaṉ pāl ōṭicceṉṟu, avaṉ pātaṅkaḷil aṭiyaṟṟa marampōl vīḻntu uri kuḻaṟavum, uṭalpataṟavum, “vēntē ! ulakit taṉi āḻi celuttukiṉṟa apayaṉukku iṭutiṟiyic coṉṉapaṭi koṭātu tavaṟiyatē ippaṭikaḷ varutaṟkuk kāraṇam. ivi apayaṉ ēviya paṭikaḷākum. yām kūṟiyavaṟṟi muṉṉamē niṉintāyilli. ippōtu namakku ivvakit tuṉpam nērntatu” eṉa oruvarpōṟ palarum kūṟi niṉṟaṉar.
“ulakuk korumuta lapayaṟ kiṭutiṟi
yuritap piyatema taracēyem
palakaṟ paṉikaḷi niṉivuṟ ṟilivaru
paṭimaṟ ṟavaṉviṭu paṭiyeṉṟē.”
“uriyiṟ kuḻaṟiyu muṭaliṟ pataṟiyu
moruvark koruvarmuṉ muṟiyiṭṭē
yariyiṟ ṟukilviḻa vaṭiyac caṉapati
yaṭiyiṟ pukaviḻu poḻutattē.”
payam eṉpati aṟiyāta vaṭakaliṅkar vēntaṉāṉa aṉanta paṉmaṉ itaṉic cevikkoḷḷutalum, vevviya taṟukaṇ vekuḷiyiṉāl neṭṭuyirttuk kikoṭṭi viyarttu eripaṟakka nōkki, “vaṇṭukaḷukkum matatti uṇṇak koṭukkum tikku yāṉikaḷippōṉṟu aḷikkiṉṟa kavinta malarkkaramuṭiya apayaṟkaṉṟi avaṉ taṇṭiṉukkum yāṉ eḷi yaṉō?” eṉak kūṟi vekuṇṭu vicālitta puyaṅkaḷ kuluṅka nakittāṉ. nakittuk kāṭu, mali, kaṭal ākiya araṇkaḷik kaliṅkarnāṭu cūḻntu kiṭakkiṉṟateṉpati aṟiyātu accōḻaṉ taṇṭu vantatu pōlum” eṉa moḻintāṉ; moḻitalum avaṉ mantirikaḷuḷ oruva ṉākiya eṅkarāyaṉ eḻuntu, “aracē! aracar kōpipparāyiṉum aṭiyavar avarukkuṟuti kūṟātirār. ēṉi vēntari vellac cayataraṉ tāṉi allatu avaṉ varutalum vēṇṭumō? avaṉ ēviya taṇṭiṉmuṉ pāṇṭiyar aivar keṭṭa kēṭṭiṉi nī kēṭṭilipōlum; porutaṟkup paṭi varutalum puṟaṅkoṭuttōṭiya cērariṉ ceytiyik kēṭṭiliyō; kaṭaliṟ ceṉṟu viḻiñatti aḻittuc cāliyik koṇṭatum taṇṭukoṇṭaṉṟō; cōḻaṉ paṭi pakittu eḻuntu vantu iṭipaṭṭatum immuṟiyaṉṟō; avaṉ pakiyōṭu pora eḻunta caḷukkiyar paṭi aḷattiṟpaṭṭa pāṭṭi aṟintiliyō; avaṉatu taṇṭat talivar naviliyit tākki avarkaḷ āyiram yāṉikaḷik koṇṭaṉaraṉṟō; iṉṉum vēntar palarip palaviṭaṅkaḷilum apayaṉ veṉṟatu tāṉ ceṉṟaṉṟit taṉ taṇṭik koṇṭaṉṟō? avaṉatu cakkaramākiya karuṇākaraṉit tali miyākak koṇṭu, avaṉ cēṉi nammit tākka varukiṉṟatu, ippōtu nī eṉṉic ciṉakkakkūṭumāyiṉum, avaṉ cēṉimuṉ nāḷi niṟkum pōtu yāṉ kūṟiyataṉ uṇmiyi nī aṟivāy,” eṉak kūṟi muṭittāṉ. kaliṅka vēntaṉ avvārttikaḷik kēṭkappeṟātu taṉṉamiccaṉi nōkki, “eṉakku etirvārtti kūṟa imiyōrum naṭuṅkuvar. kuṉṟaṉiya eṉatu puyaṅkaḷ neṭunāṭkaḷākap pōr peṟātu melintiruppati nī kaṇṭiliyō? nī uṟuti kūṟuvatiṟ piḻittāy; eṉakku uṟuti kūṟuvatu niṉperumi keṭavō? ciṟiya kaṇṇuṭiya ciṅkak kuṭṭiyi etirttal eḷiteṉṟu niṉittu yāṉi ataṉik kiṭṭi etirkka varumō? eṉatu tōḷvali vāḷvali ākiyavaṟṟic ciṟitu maṟiyātārpōla aṟiyāmi yiṉāl ivvāṟurittāy. niṉippaḷavil appaṭiyi velva taritō? ” eṉakkūṟi, “yāṉi, kutiri, tēr, kālāḷ mutaliya eṉatu nāṟ paṭikaḷum pāṭivīṭu koṇṭirukkum cōḻaṉatu paṭi etiriṟ ceṉṟu amar toṭaṅkuka,” eṉa āṇi koṭuttāṉ.
‘pōr yāṉikaḷiyum, kutirikaḷiyum, eṇṇillāta tērkaḷi yum kaṭāvik kālāṭkaḷum cerukkaḷattē celka; iṉi namakkup pōr kiṭittatu,’ eṉṟu kaliṅkattē pēroli eḻuntatu. ēḻu kaliṅkattum eḻunta periya āravāram, muḻaṅkukiṉṟa kaṭalēḻum orē kālattup poṅki eḻuntatu pōṉṟatu. matam pāyum tuḷikaḷiyuṭiya mata malikaḷ kaṭal nīruṇṭa mēkam pōṟ piḷiṟiṉa. vaḷinta mukattiṉ nuri cintap pāyntu varukiṉṟa turakaṅkaḷ kaṭalil maṟittaṭikkum tirikaḷ pōṉṟaṉa.parikaḷ viciyōṭu iḻuttuc cellum tērkaḷ at tirikaḷ mītu ōṭum tōṇikaḷ pōṉṟaṉa. olikkum vīrak kaḻal kaṭṭiya vayavariṉ muṟukiya kōpa aṉal akkaṭaliṭattē maṭutteriyum ūḻit tī pōṉṟatu. pukaḻvirumpit tam uyirit tiraṇameṉa matikkum avvīrar, kaṭaliṭattē uḻutu tiriyum cuṟāmīṉkaḷ pōṉṟaṉar. aracar iṭiyiṭiyē piṭikkiṉṟa kuṭikaḷum, iraṭṭukiṉṟa kavariyum tiriyiṉ taliyil tōṉṟum nuripōṉṟaṉa. neruṅkip piṟaḻuṅ koṭikaḷ kaṭaliṭattē viḷiyāṭum kayalmīṉkaḷ pōṉṟaṉa. vēṟpaṭikaḷi ṉalakiṉōṭu alaku paṭṭuk kalakala eṉa eḻum oli, aṟikiṉṟa tirikaḷiṉ olipōṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam, ulakip parukum oru kaṭal eṉumpaṭi kaliṅkar paṭi eḻuntatu. oruvar uṭal oruvar uṭaliṟ pukumpaṭi vīrar neruṅkiṉar. kāṇpōr añcumpaṭi neruṅkic cellum paṭiyiṉ naṭuvil oru viraliṭa veḷiyaritu. iṭi veḷiyiṉṟi neruṅkic ceṉṟa kaliṅkar paṭi aṇivakuttuk karuṇā karaṉatu paṭiyiṉetirē puli nirikaḷip pōl valimiyōṭu nirittu niṉṟaṉa; niṉṟu paṭikkalaṅkaḷi eṭum eṭum eṉṟu ārttaṉa. 
pōr
eṭumeṭu meṭumeṉa veṭuttatō
rikaloli kaṭaloli yikakkavē
viṭuviṭu viṭupari karikkuḻām
viṭumviṭu meṉumoli mikikkavē.
veruvara varicili teṟittanāṇ
vicipaṭu ticimukam veṭikkavē
ceruviṭi yiḷiyavar teḻittatōr
teḻiyula kukaḷ cevi ṭeṭukkavē.
eṟikaṭa loṭukaṭal kiṭittapō
lirupaṭi kaḷumetir kiṭikkavē
maṟitiri yoṭuniri malittapōl
varupari yoṭupari malikkavē.
kaṉavari yoṭuvari muṉittapōṟ
kaṭakari yoṭukari muṉikkavē
yiṉamuki loṭumuki letirttapō
liratamu miratamu metirkkavē.
porupuli puliyoṭu cilittapōṟ
porupaṭa roṭupaṭar cilikkavē
yariyiṉo ṭariyiṉa maṭarppapō
laracaru maracaru maṭarkkavē.
viḷikaṉal viḻikaḷiṉ muḷikkavē
miṉaloḷi kaṉaliṭi piṟakkavē
vaḷicili yurumeṉa viṭikkavē
vaṭikaṇi neṭumaḻi ciṟakkavē.
kurutiyi ṉativeḷi parakkavē
kuṭarniri nuriyeṉa mitakkavē
karituṇi paṭumuṭa laṭukkiyē
kariyeṉa virupuṭi kiṭakkavē.
paṭi eṭum eṭum eṉa eḻunta ōci kaṭaloliyik kaṭantatu. pari kaḷi viṭum viṭum, yāṉikaḷi viṭum viṭum eṉa irutiṟap paṭikaḷum kūvumōci mikuntatu. varintu kaṭṭiya villiṉ nāṇi accantarumpaṭi teṟikku mōciyāl ticāmukaṅkaḷ veṭittaṉa. irutiṟa vīrarkaḷum ataṭṭum ataṭṭukaḷ ulakic ceviṭupaṭuttiṉa. tiri eṟikiṉṟa kaṭaletiriṟ kaṭal kiṭantatu pōl iru paṭikaḷum kiṭantaṉa. tiriyōṭu tiri etirttatupōl pariyoṭu pari porutiṉa. maliyōṭu mali malivatupōl mata yāṉikaḷ malintaṉa. mukiṟ kūṭṭattōṭu mukiṟ kūṭṭam etirppatupōl iratamu miratamu metirttaṉa. valiya puliyoṭu puli etirttal pōl vīraroṭu vīrar etirttaṉar. ciṅkattōṭu ciṅkam etirttalpōl aracarumaru metirttaṉar. irupaṭi vīrariṉ kaṇkaḷilum tīppoṟi paṟantaṉa. attīyiṉiṉṟum miṉṉaloḷi piṟantatu. vaḷinta cilikaḷ iṭiyeṉa iṭittaṉa. kūrṅkaṇikaḷ nīṇṭa maḻiyeṉap poḻintaṉa. iratta āṟukaḷ perukiṉa. kuṭar nirikaḷ nuriyeṉa mitantaṉa; avvāṟṟi ṉirumaruṅkum karipōl tuṇiyuṇṭa yāṉippiṇaṅkaḷ aṭukkik kiṭantaṉa.
ivi malikaḷeṉumpaṭi maruppoṭu maruppu muṭṭi etirttup porum kaḷiṟukaḷiṉ kompiṭiyē neruppoṭu poṟikaḷ eḻuntaṉa. appoṟikaḷ niḻaṟceyyum koṭikaḷik katuviṉa. aḻaṟ katuvutaliṉ virivilaḻinta koṭic cīlikaḷ, niṉippavar niṉippataṉmuṉ nāṭṭiya putukkoṭipōl pukikkoṭikaḷi eṭuttaṉa. matam pāyum cuvaṭukaḷuḷ viṭṭu uḻutu kaṟuppēṟiya yāṉikaḷiṉ maruppukaḷ, iṭattum valattum uḷḷa kikaḷ pōṉṟaṉa. karattōṭu karam etirteṟṟi valikkum yāṉi kaḷiṉ kikaḷ maruppuṭiya malikaḷiraṇṭip piṇikka mūṅkili muṟukkit tiritta kayiṟṟi ottaṉa. kaḷattē pāyntu celkiṉṟa kutiri kaḷiṉ mukattiṟ, kaṭiya kāṟṟōṭu kūṭi ukamuṭivil cuṭarviṭṭeḻuntu ulaki uṇṇum vaṭavik kaṉali otta tī eḻuntatu. cila vīrar kaḷattē varukiṉṟa kutirikaḷi etirātu, yāṉikaḷiṉ maruppukaḷuk ketirē mārpik koṭuttu niṉṟaṉar. yāṉikaḷiṉ maruppi eṟipaṭipōṉṟu mārpil ēṟṟa vīrar mārpiṉ taḻumpukaḷ, cayamātu ceyta aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ pōṉṟaṉa. cila vīrar “ivi cayamātu mārpil aṇiyat takunta muttukkaḷ” eṉṟu kūṟi avi utirumpaṭi yāṉiyiṉ mattakaṅkaḷi vāḷāl veṭṭiṉar. kaḷattu yāṉip piṇaṅkaḷ nirittukkiṭantaṉa; paṭikaḷ cerukkaḷattē ali eṉa niri niriyē niṉṟaṉa. oppillāta viṟpaṭi akkiṉiccaṭṭi pōṉṟu eriyum neruppuṭimiyiṉ1, eri kiṉṟa mūṅkil vaṉatti ottatu. taḻalpaṭṭa mūṅkil vaṉam eppaṭiyō appaṭic caṭacaṭa eṉṉum ōci eḻavum, ampukaḷ taḻalōṭu pukiyik kakkavum, cilar villiṭattu ōyātu ampit toṭuttu nāṇi valittu niṉṟaṉar; villiṭattu ampu toṭuttuviṭap pukumiṭattu etirttavari vāḷāl veṭṭiṉar; veṭṭa, uṭalkaḷ iru tuṇṭāy viḻuntaṉa; avaṟṟuḷ oru tuṇi kuṟivitta ilakki aḻikka, viṭupaṭṭa piṟimukakkaṇi “aritu aritu” eṉṟu kūṟi ellōrum aticayikkumpaṭi parivīrariṉ muṭi kaḷiyum aṭikaḷiyum koytu vīḻttiṟṟu; aṭiyum muṭiyumaṟṟu viḻuvōr tamatu ampi mārpōṭaṇittaṉar. nāṇēṟṟiya accaraṅkaḷ viṭupaṭṭup pala vil nāṇkaḷi aṟuttaṉa. viṭucaraṅkaḷāl nāṇ tuṇivuṟṟavar, viṟkaḷi muṟitteṟipavarpōl avaṟṟi eṟintu etirttu varukiṉṟa kaḻal kaṭṭiya vīrariṉ uṭal iru vakirpaṭumpaṭi vāḷāl veṭṭi ṉar. pakivar āyutaṅkaḷ tammēṟpaṭātu eṭutta mantira vēlipōlak kaliṅkar paricikaḷi naṭṭu vittaṉar. naṭṭa paricikaḷ mītu vēlkaḷ pāyntu tiṟanta vāykaḷ, vaṭṭa niṇamatilukku vitta tuvāraṅkaḷ pōṉṟaṉa. kalakkamillāta vīrariṉ vāḷkaḷ etirtta cūrariṉ kiyiṭattuḷḷa ulakkiyiṉ ucciyiṟ pāyntu tittapōtu uḻuṅ kalappi pōṉṟaṉa. curuṇṭu viḻum matayāṉiyiṉ karam, taṉmītu valiya caram tittapōtu cakkarap paṭiyi ottatu. koṭiya yāṉiyiṉ mattakattiṉiṉṟum cori yum muttukaḷ, vīramātukkuc coriyum maṅkalap poripōṉṟaṉa. parici yuṭaṉ tuñcikkiṭakkum vīrar tērccillōṭu kiṭakkum malipōṉṟaṉar. vīrar ēviya cakkarap paṭikaḷ, maṟitteṟinta taṇṭukaḷuḷ nuḻintu kiyiṭattēntiya kūr maḻukkaḷ pōṉṟaṉa. piṇaṅkaḷōṭu āṭum pēykaḷ, niruttamāṭa āṭṭuvikkum niruttakārari ottaṉa. oṭṭakaṅkaḷ, yāṉikaḷiyum kutirikaḷiyum vāḷiṉāl vīci eṟintu ceyyum pōri viṭṭu nīṅka maṉamiṉṟit tirumpuvapōl mīṇṭaṉa. viḷaṅkum irattak kaṭaliṟ piḷiṟi viḻum yāṉik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ, oliyuṭiya kaṭal nīruṭ paṭinta mēkatti ottaṉa. yāṉiyiṉ kikaḷi vāḷiṉāl veṭṭit tōḷiliṭṭa vīrar turuttiyit tōḷiliṭṭu nīrviṭum turuttiyāḷari ottaṉar. pakivar villiṭattuc carantoṭuttu viṭutalum, ampillāta cila vīrar mārpiṟ titta ampip piṭuṅkic cāpattiṟṟoṭut teytaṉar. virutarkaḷ kutiriyiṉ mārpiṟ cavaḷap paṭiyi aḻutti uyarttiya tōṟṟam, cayamātukkuk kaḷattē eṭutta koṭiyi ottatu. iru toṭi kaḷumaṟṟuk kiṭakkum vayavar, etirē varukiṉṟa yāṉiyiṉ vali keṭumpaṭi oru toṭiyi viṭṭeṟintu oru toṭiyi iṉi eṟitaṟku iṭṭu vittaṉar. otta palamuṭiya iru vīrar, oruvar mārpil oruvar uṭi vāḷi ēṟṟi oruvar pōlvīḻtaliṉ periya ārppeḻuntatu. porum vīrar cilar, tam mārpik kuttak kaviḻntu varukiṉṟa yāṉikaḷ mītu aṭivittēṟi mītirukkum cūrariṉ talikaḷi arintaṉar; avi viḻumpōtu “aṟi” eṉṟu akkaḷiṟṟiṉukkuc cattamiṭṭaṉa. cilar, tamatu mārpik kuttak kaviḻkiṉṟa pōr yāṉiyiṉ mattakatti aṭittaṉar. piṉ atu taṅkaḷiṟeṉṟaṟintu nāṇip paṭiñar ikaḻvāreṉa añci, ataṉik kollātu viṭukilar. ivvāṟu irutiṟappaṭikaḷum viṭātum iḷikkātum ativīrac ceyalkaḷik kāṭṭi otta palattoṭu pōr ceytalum pōri muṭittu vākicūṭa niṉintu vaṇṭiyar aracaṉum, aracarkkut talivaṉum vicayataraṉatu mantiriyumākiya ulakam pukaḻum karuṇākaraṉ oru kiyum iraṇṭu kompumuṭiya matamali oṉṟu ukittup pōrmukattuḷ nuḻintāṉ; pukutalum, avaṉ paṭikaḷ uṟcākam mikuntu orumukamāka muṉ ceṉṟaṉa. tēvarkaḷum appōrik kāṇa muntiṉar; kaliṅkariṉ matayāṉikaḷ tuṇipaṭṭu viḻuntaṉa; niritta kutirikaḷōṭu tērkaḷ oṭintaṉa; vīrar talikaḷ kuvintu malipōl vāṉil vaḷarntaṉa; iratta veḷḷam, olikkiṉṟa kaṭalpōṟ pāyntatu; kuṭarkaḷ iratta veḷḷattiṉmītu parantu nīntiṉa. kutiri celuttum kōlkaḷum cēṇaṅkaḷum citaṟikkiṭantaṉa. ticikaḷōṭu aṭṭakirikaḷum atirntaṉa. eṅkum timilakumilamāyiṟṟu. puraciyiliruntu āyiram mata yāṉikaḷik koṇarntu poruvōm eṉṟu āṇi kūṟiya ēḻu kaliṅka aracaṉ vīram kuṉṟiyatu. avaṉ vīrar amaril etir niṟkamāṭṭātu ōṭi otuṅkiṉar; alatikulatiyā yaccaṅkoṇṭu, “ippaṭi eṉṉa māyiyō maṟaliyō ūḻiyiṉ kaṭiyō” eṉṟalaṟi aṟinta malik kukikaḷiṟ patuṅkavum, ariya pilaṅkaḷiṭi maṟiyavum, aṭaviyiṟ karappavum citintōṭiṉar. cilar kumpiṭṭu niṉṟaṉar. cilar kaṭaliṟ kutit toḷittaṉar. cilar uṭaliṟ kariyip pūci maṟintaṉar. cilar vaḻitēṭip pilaṅkaḷiṟ pukuntaṉar. ivvāṟu oruvar pōṉavaḻi oruvar pōkātu karantaṉar. iruvar oruvaḻi pōkātu oruvar oruvarāka ōṭa muntiṉar. ōṭumiṭattut tamatu niḻalikkaṇṭu, tammit turattic cellum cōḻa vīrareṉṟu añciṉar. cilar nām arukar arukar eṉak kūṟi apayam apayam eṉṟu naṭuṅkiṉar. mukil atirvatu pōl atirntu eḻum cōḻaṉatu yāṉip paṭiyip pōṉṟiruṇṭa kukikaḷil nuḻintōr, pōriṭattu namatu mutukukaḷ ceyta upakāram itu eṉakkūṟiṉar. iraṇṭu kulattukkum nīṅkāta ciṟappāka viḷaṅkum vicayataraṉ eḷitil veṟṟi māli puṉiya, ēḻu kaliṅkap paṭiyum orumukamāymuṟintu apayamiṭṭatu.
ēḻu kaliṅkattiyum karuṇākaraṉ aḻitta nāḷ ēḻu kaliṅkar ōr āṭiyi uṭuttaṉar1. ivvāṟu kaliṅkar ōṭap paṭivīrariṉ kippaṭṭa yāṉi kutiri mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṇitturippavar yār?
“puṇṭoṟum kuruti pāyap poḻitaru kaṭamum pāya 
vaṇṭoṭum paruntiṉōṭum vaḷippuṇṭa kaḷiṟanēkam”
“oṭṭaṟap paṭṭapōri lūrpavar tammi vīcik 
kaṭṭaṟut tavarpō ṉiṉṟu kaṭṭuṇṭa kaḷiṟanēkam”
“varicila pulikaḷōṭum vantukaṭ ṭuṇṭa vēpō
laricarun tāmuṅ kaṭṭuṇ ṭakappaṭṭa kaḷiṟanēkam”
katiyuṭiya kutiriyum, tērum, navanitiyum, makaḷirum eṉṟu avarkaḷ kikkoṇṭavaṟṟi aḷaviṭal aritu. avaṟṟi ellām kavarntapiṉ karuṇākaraṉ taṉ paṭi vīrariyum oṟṟariyum nōkki, “ēḻu kaliṅkaratu aracaṉiyum paṟṟikkoṇṭu peyartaṟku avaṉ irukku miṭatti aṟintu varumiṉ” eṉak kūṟiṉāṉ. kūṟutalum olikkum kaṭal pōṉṟa vīrarum oṟṟarum kaṭitu ceṉṟu malikaḷilum vaṉaṅkaḷilum muṉi varkaḷ irukkum paṉṉacālikaḷilum taṭavip pārttut tirumpivantu, “avaṉatu aṭiccuvaṭum peṟṟilō” meṉak kūṟiṉar. piṉ cila oṟṟarkaḷ vantu, “avaṉ irukkumiṭatti aṟintēm. oru maliyiṭattic cārntu koṭiya cila vīrarik kāvalākak koṇṭu uṟikiṉṟāṉ. avviṭatti aṭital aritu” eṉa viḷampiṉar. uṭaṉē avaṉ vīrar, kaliṅka nāṭṭil vēṟu maliyum kaṭalum nāṭum tēṭuvataṟku illi eṉumpaṭi avaṟṟi ellām tēṭic cūriyaṉ attamaṉakiriyi aṭiyum aḷavil kaliṅka varacaṉ irunta veṟpi aṇukiṉar.
aṇuki, tōlāta kaḷiṟuṭiya apayaṉ paṉṟi vēṭṭiyāṭaṟku vaḷittaṭitta vēliyik kāppār pōla vēlālum villālum vēli kōli maliyi viṭiyumaḷavum kāttu niṉṟu kaliṅkavēntaṉi akap paṭuttiṉar. immali iyalpākavē civappāka oḷipeṟṟatō? aṉṟi itu cūriyaṉ utayamākum utayakiritāṉō? eṉṟu kaṇṭār aiyuṟumpaṭi kaliṅkariṉ irattam āṟākap perukiṟṟu. appōtu tukilurinta kaliṅkar mācupūcit tamatu mayirip paṟitteṟintu “nām amaṇar” eṉak kūṟip piḻittaṉar. anēkar, cili nāṇi munnūlāka maṭittut tarittu, “yām kaṅkiyāṭac celpavarkaḷ; vitivacattāl immaliyil akappaṭṭuk koṇṭōm” eṉak kūṟip piḻittaṉar. anēkar kuruti tōynta koṭic cīlikaḷik kāviyāṭiyāka uṭuttut taliyi moṭṭiyaṭittuk koṇṭu, “uṭiyik kaṇṭāl eṅkaḷip putta reṉṟaṟiyīrō” eṉak kūṟi apayamiṭṭōṭiṉar. anēkar, taṅkaḷ yāṉikaḷiṉ kaḻuttiṟ kaṭṭiyirunta maṇikaḷi aviḻttuk kiyiṟ piṭittuk koṇṭu, “aiyā! nāṅkaḷ tāḷampiṭittup pāṭi āṭum teluṅkap pāṇarkaḷ: cēṉikaḷ maṭintu kiṭakkiṉṟa kaliṅkap pōrkkaḷattik kaṇṭu tikittu niṟkiṉ ṟōm” eṉac collip piḻittōṭiṉar.
“vēṭattāṟ kuṟiyātu munnū lāka
veñcilināṇ maṭittiṭṭu vitiyāṟ kaṅki
yāṭappōn takappaṭṭēṅ kāttōm peṉṟeṉ
1ṟapitāviṭ ṭuyir piḻittā ranēka rāṅkē”
“kuṟiyākak kurutinīrk kōṭi yāṭi
koṇṭuṭuttup pōrttuttaṅ kuñci muṇṭit
taṟiyīrō cākkayari yuṭikaṇ ṭāleṉ
ṟappiramaṇ ṇiyamiṭuvā ranēka rāṅkē”
“cēṉimaṭi kaḷaṅkaṇṭu tikittu niṉṟēn
teluṅkarē meṉṟucilik kaliṅkar taṅka
ḷāṉimaṇi yiṉittāḷam piṭittup pāṭi
yuṭippāṇa reṉappiḻittā ranēka rāṅkē”
ivvāṟu tappi ōṭiyavarkaḷē piḻittaṉar; kaliṅka ōviyar cuvaril eḻutiya ōviyaṅkaḷum piḻittaṉa; maṟṟavarkaḷi ellām cōḻa vīrar piṭittiḻuttu aṟutteṟintārkaḷ. kaṭalpōṉṟa kaliṅkaratu paṭiyi veṉṟu cayattampam nāṭṭi matayāṉikaḷum valiya kutirikaḷum kuvitaṉa muṅkoṇṭu, viḷaṅkukiṉṟa vāḷuṭiya apayaṉ aṭiyi vaṇṭayark karacaṉākiya toṇṭimāṉ aruḷōṭu cūṭiṉāṉ. ippōrkkaḷam, tēvācura yuttaṅkaḷ uṇṭeṉṟa oḻiyāta pēcci aṭakkiṟṟu.

1kaḷaṅkāṭṭiyatu
“iṟivī! kolikkaḷattik kaṇṭaruḷuti” eṉak kūḷi vēṇṭu talum, kālakaṇṭaṉ makiḻkiṉṟa amuti otta kāḷi pōrkkaḷatti aṇuki, “ikkoṭiya pōrkkaḷam eṉṉē!” eṉṟu aticayamuṟṟu ap pēyk kaṇaṅkaḷ, akkaḷattuḷḷavaṟṟik kaṇṭaruḷumpaṭi kāṭṭik kūṟukiṉṟāḷ:
uṭalmītu paṭṭa kāyaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟuṅ kuruti coriyap piṉṉaṅ kālōṭu irattaveḷḷattil tuṭittuc cellum marakkalaṅkaḷ pōṉṟaṉa, pārmiṉ. uyarnta naṟkutiri mītiṭṭa kalaṇi cāyumpaṭiyāy niṇac cēṟṟiṟ kālkaḷ putintu niriyē niṟkum kutirikaḷ, kuruti veḷḷatti aṇikaṭṭak kutirip pāyccalāka niṟuttiya taṟikaḷi ottaṉa, kāṇmiṉ. viruntiṉarum vaṟiyōrum neruṅkiyirun tuṇṇa muka malarum mēlōr pōṟ, paruntiṉamum kaḻukiṉamum paṭintuṇṇa, malarnta paṅkaya vataṉaṅ kaḷik kāṇmiṉ. cāmaḷavum piṟark kutavāri virumpic cārntavar kaḷip pōla vīraruṭaliṟṟaṅkum āvi pōmaḷavum avararukē iruntu piṉṉum viṭṭu nīṅkāta pala narikkūṭṭaṅkaḷik kāṇmiṉ. cāyntu viḻum matayāṉikaḷuṭaṉ cāyntu kurutiyiṟ paṭiyum koṭikaḷ, kaṇavaruṭaṉ eri pukuntu uṭaṉmaṭiyuṅ kaṟpuṭi mātari ottaṉa kāṇamiṉ. kaṟpuṭi ariviyar cilar, taṅkaṇavaruṭaṉ tāmuñ celvataṟku, ‘eṅkaṇavar kiṭanta iṭam eṅkē’ eṉṟu pēykaḷik kēṭpar; taṭavippārppar; iṭā kiṉikaḷiyuṅ kēṭpar, pārmiṉ. taṉ kaṇavaṉ vāy maṭittuk kiṭattalik kaṇṭu, ‘maṇi atarattu ētēṉum vaṭuvuṇṭaṉiyō? nī itaḻ maṭittuk kiṭattaṟkuk kāraṇameṉṉa?’ eṉṟu kūvip pulampi āvi cōrkiṉṟa vaḷik kāṇmiṉ. pūmātu taṉ kaṇavaṉ uṭalit tāṅkāmal taṉ karat tāṟṟāṅki, viṇṇāṭṭariviyar avaṉic cērvataṉmuṉ taṉ āviyi uṭaṉ viṭuvāḷik kāṇmiṉ. porun taṭakkiyil vāḷeṅkē? maṇi mārpeṅkē? pōrmukattevarvariṉum puṟaṅkoṭāta periyavayirattō ḷeṅkē; eṉṟu payiraviyik kēṭpāḷik kāṇmiṉ; kāṇmiṉ.
“porutaṭakki vāḷeṅkē maṇimār peṅkē
pōrmukat tevarvariṉum puṟaṅko ṭāta
paruvayirat tōḷeṅkē yeṅkē yeṉṟu
payiraviyik kēṭpāḷik kāṇmiṉ kāṇmiṉ”
pāyntuvarum kutiriyip piṭittu āḷi āḷōṭu aṭittup puṭikkap puṇṇiṉ nīrōṭit teṟittuk karumēkam civanta maḻi poḻivatip pārmiṉ kaḷattē neruṅkiya niṇappōrvi mūṭak karuṅ kākam veṇkākamāka māṟiya orupōtumillāta putumiyik kāṇmiṉ. neruṅkiya tērkkoṭiñci tāmari moṭṭākavum, irattam nīrākavum, viḻukiṉṟa koṭi iliyākavum, acceṅkaḷam tāmarip poykiyāka viḷaṅkuvatik kāṇmiṉ. ampukaḷum vēlkaḷum moyttu viḻātu niṟkum vīrar kaḻikkūttar kayiṟṟāl kaṭṭic cāyātu niṟkum mūṅkil pōṉ ṟaṉar. pārmiṉ uṭaṟkuṟiyiṉmītu ēṟiya ampu putiyumpaṭi mēliruntu ciṟaku virittāṭum paruntiṉ āṭṭattik kāṇmiṉ. varukiṉṟa paki variṉ cēṉi tam paṭimītu vantuṟātapaṭi vāḷvīrar, tam uyir keṭumpaṭi vīraṅ kāṭṭic ceñcōṟṟukkaṭaṉ kaḻitta kimāṟṟik kāṇmiṉ. yāṉi vīrar etirttapōtu aṟṟu eḻuntāṭukiṉṟa talikaḷ, vīramātu eṟintāṭum ammāṉik kāykaḷ pōṉṟaṉa pārmiṉ. etirkoḷkiṉṟa tēvariṉ vimāṉaṅkaḷiṟ ṟēvarāy ēṟum vīrar, eṇṇutaṟkariya anēkarātaliṉ, viṇṇulakattilum ivvakip perunāḷ illi eṉa attēvar aiyuṟutalik kāṇmiṉ. kaliṅkaratu yāṉi coriyum matam kaṭalpōṟ peruka atit taṭuttum, parikaḷākiya tiriyi alittum amarceyta kaliṅkaratu uṭaliṉiṉṟum peruki irattak kaṭalmītu kavaripōla nuri parantōṭutalik kāṇmiṉ. ulakik kākkum vicayataraṉ 1muṉṉoru pōtu periya kaṭalmītu aṇiyiṭa oppilāta villi viḷittapōtu, kuraṅkiṉam kaṭaliṭattē kuvitta malikkūṭṭampōlak kariya yāṉip piṇaṅkaḷ avvirattak kaṭaliṟ kiṭappatik kāṇmiṉ. pakivar viṭutta ampu, pariciyiyum kavacattiyum ūṭuruvi mārpip piḷakka ‘ivaṉ viṟṟiṟami eṉṉē’eṉakkūṟik kimmaṟittu viḻum vīrarik kāṇmiṉ. tēvarkaḷāki mēl eḻukiṉṟa vīrar māttiramallar, kaṇṇimippu nīṅka mukamalarntu kiṭakkum uṭalkaḷum tēvari ottaṉa pārmiṉ. piṟipōṉṟa maruppuṭiya yāṉikaḷ vālum taḷiyum tuṇiyuṇṇat taliyaṟṟuk kiṭakkum tuṇṭaṅkaḷ kollaṉ uliyil irumpaṭikkum cammaṭṭi kaḷip pōṉṟaṉa, pārmiṉ. vāyiṟ pukunta vēlik kiyāṟpaṟṟi nilattiṟ cariyum vīrar vāttiya mūtuvōri ottaṉar kāṇmiṉ. mār piṭattup pāynta nīṇṭa vēlippiṭuṅki nilattē ūṉṟit tērmēl niṟpōr, muṉṉē irattaveḷḷam kiṭattaliṉ paṭavu oṭṭum toḻilōr pōṉṟaṉar pārmiṉ. vāyalampukiṉṟa irattattōṭu niṇattik kavarntu paṟakkum paruntip piṉṉoru kūriya nakamuṭiya periya paruntu piṉ toṭarntu, mukilkaḷ ōṭukiṉṟa akaṉṟa ākāya vītiyiṟ caṇṭiyiṭṭup piṇattip paṟikka valiya vāy kiḻintu atu nilattil vīḻvatip pārmiṉ. caturaṅka cēṉikkut talivaṉip pōrkkaḷattē vanta periya vayiṟuṭiya pūtam aruntiya periya taliyic cumantu vayiṟūti oru perum mukil pōl varutalik kāṇmiṉ. mutiya malimītēṟi iruppōri otta yāṉi vīrar pōrkkaḷattut tam mutuku vaṭuppaṭum eṉa añci niṉṟu mārpiṭattē vaṭuppaṭṭuk kiṭattalik kāṇmiṉ.
“cāturaṅkat talivaṉippōrk kaḷattil vanta 
taḻivayiṟṟup pūtantā ṉarunti mikka
cāturaṅkat talicumantu kamañcūṟ koṇṭu
taṉippaṭuṅkā reṉavarumat taṉmikāṇmiṉ”
“mutukuvaṭup paṭiyirukku meṉṉa niṟku
muṉikkaḷiṟṟōr cerukkaḷattu muntu taṅkaṇ
mutukuvaṭup paṭumeṉṟa vaṭuvi yañci 
muṉṉamvaṭup paṭṭavari yiṉṉaṅ kāṇmiṉ”

1kūḻaṭutal
“pōrkkaḷam muṟṟum kāṭṭuvataṟku muṭivataṉṟu; āḷi aṭu taṟkuk kaviḻukiṉṟa matayāṉiyiṉ irattañ coriyak kuḷam maṭitirun tatupōṟ kumiḻi viṭṭup perukum irattattiṟ ṟōyntu kūḻaṭuṅkaḷ” eṉṟu aṇaṅku collak kaṇaṅkaḷ kumpiṭṭuk kūḻaṭat toṭaṅkiṉa.
kuṟumōṭī! niṇamāli! kūṭivayiṟī! kūreyiṟī! nīli! māṭī! kutir vayiṟī! ellīrum kūḻaṭa vāruṅkaḷ. yāṉi maruppiṉāṟ palli viḷakku miṉ; ataṉ paḻuvelumpi oṭittu nākki vaḻiyumiṉ; ampiṉ vāyāki ya ukirkoḷḷiyāl nakaṅkaḷik kaḷimiṉ; pāy kaḷiṟṟiṉ matamākiya eṇṇeyi oḻuka oḻukat talikku vimiṉ, eṇṇey pōka veṇmūḷi eṉṉum kaḷimaṇṇit tēyttu iratta maṭuviṟ kūṭṭa mākap pāyntu nīnti yāṭumiṉ; iratta maṭu attaṉiyum ampum vēlum kuntamum kiṭappatāl ikkaṭṭukkup payantōr kariyiṭattē iruntu kuḷimiṉ; āḻnta iratta maṭuvil nīnti neṭunēram viḷiyāṭātu kariyēṟi, vīḻnta kaliṅkariṉ niṇa āṭiyi virittu virittu uṭumiṉ; matayāṉikaḷiṉ kimpurikaḷi aḻakiya ki vaḷiyalkaḷāka aṇimiṉ; kutirikaḷiṉ cataṅki cērttiya mālikaḷi, mutti uḷ maṇiyāka iṭṭa pātacaraṅkaḷāka aṇimiṉ; ceruviṭattu vīra reṟinta periya vaḷi taṭikaḷi, vēṇṭiyaḷavum vāyi nekiḻntu, 2viṭukampikaḷāka aṇimiṉ; periya yāṉiyiṉ kariya karaṅkaḷik 3karu nāṇākak kaṭṭumiṉ; iraṭṭi muracameṭuttu naṭuvē vāḷiṉ piṭiyic ceruki iraṭṭi muracameṭuttu naṭuvē vāḷiṉ piṭiyic ceruki iraṭṭi 4vāḷiyāka aṇimiṉ; paṭṭa kutiriyiṉ kuḷampukaḷi vākuvalayamāka aṇimiṉ; eṟintu kiṭakkum puriyuṭiya caṅkukaḷi ēkāvaliyāka aṇimiṉ; ciṉantu poruta vīrariṉ kaṇmaṇiyum yāṉi mattaka muttuṅkoṇṭu mayiriṟ kōttu aḻakiya mālikaḷāka aṇimiṉ; iṉṉum palavāṟāka aṇikaḷic cumatti alaṅ karittuk koṇṭiruppōmāyiṉ poḻutu pōyviṭum; paci atikarikkum. ātaliṉ paṇi pūṇuvati ivvaḷavil niṟutti uṇpataṟkākiya camiya lukku āyattañ ceyyuṅkaḷ. periya uṭaluṭiya matayāṉiyiṉ piṇa mākiya malimītu valiya kaḻukiṉ ciṟakāl vēynta aḻakiya pantariṉkīḻ aṭukkaḷiyik koḷḷuṅkaḷammā; yāṉikaḷ poḻikiṉṟa matattāl nilam meḻukip poṭintutirnta mutti ulakkimēl vittiḻuttuk kōlamiṭumiṉ; matayāṉikaḷiṉ mattakaṅkaḷi aṭuppākak koṇṭu virivākac camiyuṅkaḷammā; koṟṟa vāḷi vīrarōccak kuṭaroṭu taliyum, kālum aṟṟu vīḻnta āṉiyākiya pāṉiyi aṭuppil viyuṅkaḷammā; veṇtayirum centayirum kalantu kiṭakkum miṭākkaḷ pōṟ kiṭakkum vīrariṉ, mūḷiyākiya kuḷirnta tayiriyum īraliyum periya miṭākkaḷiṟ koḷḷuṅkaḷammā; kolkiṉṟa yāṉiyākiya miṭāvuṭ kutiriyiṉ kurutiyik kūḻaṭutaṟku uliyāka vāruṅkaḷ; kaḷattē tuḷḷi vīḻnta kutiriyiṉ veṇ pallākiya uḷḷiyiyum kiḷḷiyiṭṭu nakaṅkaḷākiya uppiyum iṭumiṉ; viṇṇulakaṭiyiṉum kaṇṇiṉiṉṟum nīṅkāta vīrariṉ kōpākkiṉiyākiya neruppi mūṭṭumiṉ; vēlum ampum kuntamum eṟitaṭikaḷumākiya viṟaku taṭikaḷi muṟitterimiṉ; kallik kaṟittup pal muṟintu kaviḻntu vīḻnta kaliṅkar tam pallit takarttup paḻavariciyāka eṭuṅkaḷ; cuviyit tarum mikunta kūḻukku iṭum ariciyik kaṟipōkat tīṭṭuṅkaḷ; kaṇṇuṭinta muracaṅ kaḷi uralkaḷākak koḷḷuṅkaḷ; ivvuralkaḷiṉ ellā ariciyiyum koṭṭik kollum yāṉiyiṉ tanta ulakkiyi ōṅki iṭṭuc calukku mulukkeṉak kuttuṅkaḷ.
“kallik kaṟittup paṉmuṟintu
kaviḻntu viḻunta kaliṅkar tam
pallit takarttup paḻavarici
yākap paṇṇik koḷḷīrē”
“inta vuraṟka ṇivvarici
yellām peytukol lāṉit 
tanta vulakki taṉiyōccic
calukku mulukkeṉak kuttīrē.”

1vaḷḷippāṭṭu
vācaṉi poruntiya māliyaṇinta cayatuṅkaṉākiya eṅkaḷ talivaṉatu vāḷvaliyāl, ematu melivu nīṅkumpaṭi piṇaṅkaḷit tanta nācciyip pāṭīr; periya tiruvuṭiyāḷip pāṭīr; katiyōṭu kūṭiya uyarnta pariyuṭiya vicayataraṉākiya kāviri nāṭuṭiyavaṉatu iru tōḷkaḷ ulakic cumantatip pāṭīr; aravu cumi tavirntatip pāṭīr; irācāti irācaṉākiya vicayataraṉatu vāraṇam ivviṭattē mataṅkoḷḷa avviṭattup pāṇṭiyar pōrkkuṭintōṭiṉamiyip pāṭīr; cērar veruvi ōṭiṉamiyip pāṭīr; vaṇaṅkiya cērar maṇimuṭiyiyum pāṇṭiyar tirumuṭiyiyum iṭaṟiya cēvaṭikaḷip pāṭīr; eṅkaḷ peru māṉatu tiruvaṭiyip pāṭīr; viḷaṅkukiṉṟa nīṇṭa vēluṭiya vāḷa payaṟku vaṭanāṭṭavar tiṟiyaḷitta kaḷiṟu varumpaṭi pāṭīr; kaṭalpōṉṟa matam nāṟuvatip pāṭīr; kaṭal ciṟiteṉṉumpaṭi vaṭṭitta koṟṟak kuṭiyuṭiya paṇṭita cōḻaṉatu malarppātaṅkaḷiṟ pakivar aṭin tami pāṭīr; avaṉatu cilivali vāḷvalikaḷip pāṭīr; eppakalilum 2veḷḷaṇi nāḷilum nilamakaḷip niḻaṟ ceyyum koṟṟak kuṭiyip pāṭīr; vaṇṭi vaḷam patiyip pāṭīr; malliyiyum kacciyiyum pāṭīr; pallavar tōṉṟalām karuṇākaraṉip pāṭīr; vēḻa nirikaḷik koṉṟu paraṇiyi nammaracaṉukkuc cūṭṭiya karuṇākaraṉip pāṭīr; toṇṭiyar vēntaṉip pāṭīr.
tuliyiliṭṭu niṟuttut tiṟiyi vittu vicayataraṉ puruvattuk kaṭipārttut talivaṇaṅkum katir muṭikaḷ nūṟāyiram. ematu aracaṉākiya mutalvaṉ cūṭum muṭi oṉṟē. avaṉ aṭicūṭum muṭikaḷi eṇṇiṉ āyiram nūṟāyiramākum. muṭiyiṉāl vaḻipaṭṭuc coṉṉa tiṟiyiṭāta vēntariṉ aṭikaḷ mitittōṭiya ariya malikaḷ nūṟāyiram. avaṉ mantirimār irukkum vāyiliṭattup pārvēntar paṭum ciṟumi nūṟāyiram. pōr tāṅkum kaḷiṟuṭiya vicayataraṉ iraṇṭu puyam pārit tāṅkap poṟi tavirtta pāmpiṉ talikaḷ āyiram; nāṉku kaṭalkaḷi yum kavitta kuṭiyuṭiya vicayataraṉ amutameḻap pāṟkaṭalik kaṭin taruḷum paṇitta tōḷkaḷ āyiram. nilavēntar talikaḷāl iraṇṭu tāḷkaḷiyum tāṅkukiṉṟa vicayataraṉ taṉatu iraṇṭu tōḷkaḷāl muṉ tuṇitta vāṇaṉatu tōḷkaḷ āyiram. mukapaṭām pōrtta kaḷiṟṟapayaṉ kiruṭṭiṇaṉākat turiyōtaṉātikaḷiṭam tūtu naṭantapōtu cakkaram mutaliya paṭikaḷ tāṅkiya aḻakiya tōḷkaḷāyiram.1
pallaricikaḷ ellām iṉik kūḻukkiṭattakka paḻavarici āyiṉa; 2callavaṭṭam eṉṉum cuḷakāṟ ṟaviṭupōkap puṭiyīr; kikaḷiṉāl nilam meḻukik kaliṅkariṉ aḻakiya ampaṟāt tūṇikaḷ nāḻiyākat tūṇimā aḷavuṅkaḷ; kik kavacaṅkaḷic ciṟiyavum ampaṟāttūṇikaḷip periyavum kūṭikaḷākak koṇṭu uraliliṭṭa ariciyi iṟakki uli yiṭattē iṭuṅkaḷ; 3paraṇikkūḻ poṅki vaḻiyātu, kiyik kāmpāka iṟukkiya periya kutirik kuḷampākiya akappiyāṟ ṟuḻāvuṅkaḷ; kūḻ aṭuppiliruttu kotittatu iṉippōtum; uppuk kāṇpataṟku eṉakkum vārttu nīṅkaḷum uḷḷaṅkiyil ovvōr tuḷi vārttuk koḷ ḷuṅkaḷ; aḻal, kiyic cuṭātirukka neruppi avittuk kit tuṭuppi ṉāṟ cuḻalac cuḻalap pakkamellān tuḻāvuṅkaḷ; ikkūḻiṉ patattik kiyiṉāṟ ṟoṭṭuppāruṅkaḷ; itaṉ patamuñ cuviyum muṉ uṇṭa kūḻkaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum mika naṉṟu.
iṉi itaṉi iṟakka vāruṅkaḷ; eṭuttuk kiyiṟ kaviḻttuk koḷḷāmaṟ kutirimītiṭṭa tuṇikaḷi irumaruṅkum aṇṭap piṭittu metuvāka aṭuppiṉiṉṟum iṟakkuṅkaḷ. oru vāykoṇṭu kuṭikka itu toliyāteṉṟu añci niṉṟīrāyiṉ, ikkūḻuṇṇa āyiramvāy vēṇṭumō? venta irumpil nīrit teḷittatu pōl uṇṭu paciyāl venteriyum inta uṭampi nāvāṟ ṟōykkil ellāk kūḻuñ cuvaṟātō? paṇṭum itupōṉṟa mikunta paraṇik kūḻi ivvulakiṟ kaṇṭaṟiyēm; nīr coṉṉa upāyattik kikkoṇṭāl uṇṭu mikātu; kotikkiṉṟa periya iratta āṟṟil vēṇṭiya taṇṇīri yāṉiyiṉ kumpaṅkaḷilē mukanteṭuttuk kuḷira vittukkoḷḷuṅkaḷ; maṭinta kaḷiṟṟiṉ vālākiya kūṭṭu māṟṟiṉāṟ cuṟṟi alakiṭumiṉ; alakiṭṭu ali eḻukiṉṟa iratta nīrit teḷittu, uṇṇum pāttiraṅkaḷi vikka nilattic camittuk koḷḷuṅkaḷ; pōr vēntariṉ kēṭakaṅkaḷit tiruppit tirukaṇiyākap parappumiṉ; pārvēntar maṇṭi ōṭukaḷip pala pāttiraṅkaḷākak koḷḷumiṉ; iṟanta kaliṅkariṉ poṟ paricikaḷip poṟpāttiraṅkaḷākak koḷḷuṅkaḷ; viḻunta muttuk kuṭikaḷi veḷḷip pāttiraṅkaḷākak koḷḷuṅkaḷ; nilattic cuttañceytu koṇṭu nīvir nīṇṭa kiyuṭiya yāṉiyiṉ paṇitta kālākiya kalaṅ kaḷi atikam kūḻkoḷḷum pāttiraṅkaḷākak koḷḷuṅkaḷ; kōpam mūṇṭu ārkkum vīrariṉ viḻikkaṉalum niṇamum, aṇaṅkiṉ aruḷ peṟṟārkkup pakal viḷakkum pāvāṭiyumākak koḷḷuṅkaḷ; aḻakākak kalaṅkaḷip parappip panti pantiyāka nirittiruntuṇṇak kūḻi vāruṅkaḷ; kaṅki koṇṭa purattiṉ matiṟku appuṟattē pakivar ciram pōy viḻuntu iṅkē kiṭakkum talikaḷi akappikaḷākak koḷḷuṅkaḷ.
maṇalūriṟ pāṇṭiyarōṭu porutapōtu aṭṭa paraṇikkūḻip paṭitta pēykaḷē! pantipantiyāy irukkum pēykaḷukkuk kūḻi vāruṅkaḷ; niṟinta cuviyuṭiya kūḻik kaṇṭu, 1“pakavati! piṭcāntēki” eṉṟu picci kēṭkum pirāmaṇappēykkuk kūḻi vāruṅkaḷ; uyir kaḷik kollāta camaṇap pēykaḷ orupōtu uṇpaṉa; avi uṇṇa, mayirillātu niṇattukilāl vaṭittu vāruṅkaḷ; tōḷi maṟittu niṟkum puttap pēykkuk kaḻuttumaṭṭum niṟintu nākkuḻaṟumpaṭi maṇam māṟāta kañciyāka vāruṅkaḷ; pōrāṭṭiṉ uṟuppaṉittum koytiṭṭa kūḻi, veḷḷāṭṭuk kuṭṭikaḷit tiṉṟu vaṟṟiya periya pēykku vāruṅkaḷ; kūḻukku āci mikunta kaḷappēy eṭuttoḷittatāl kalattit taṭavik kāṇātu araṟṟum kuruṭṭup pēyiṉ kikkuk kūḻi vāruṅkaḷ; varuntit taṉ paci yuṇartti, miṭā niṟinta kūḻi viralāṟ cuṭṭikkāṭṭik kiyāṟ pēcum ūmip pēyiṉ kalamniṟiyak kūḻ vāruṅkaḷ; aṭiyēṉukkup paciyālaṭitta kātukaḷ tiṟantaṉaveṉṟu kaṭavāyit tuṭittu nakkic cuḻaṉṟu niṉṟa cūlkoṇṭa pēykku iṉṉuñ kūḻ vāruṅkaḷ; pollāta ōṭṭik kalattē kūḻ puṟattoḻukak kaviḻttu nōkki ellāṅ koṭṭit tikittirukkum ūmippēykkuk kūḻi vāruṅkaḷ. tutikkiyiṉ tuṇṭip palliṉmītu nērāka niṟutti vittu, kiyiṉ nutikkuk kūḻi vāreṉṉum eḷiya pēykkuk kūḻi vāruṅkaḷ; iṟicciyit tiṉṟu, kūḻ muḻutiyum tāṉ kuṭittuttaṉ kaṇavaṉ kuṭiyāṉeṉak kulukkaṭikkum kūttip pēykku iṉṉum vāruṅkaḷ; paraṇikkūḻ aṭutaṟku, muṉ kaḷattik kaṇṭu vantu coṉṉa pēykku muṉvārttu nāmirukkum ūrppēykaḷukkuk kalam muṭṭa vāruṅkaḷ; iraviṟ kaṇṭa kaṉāvuritta pēykku iṉṟikkaṉṟi nāḷikkum kutirit tōlākiya (potikaṭṭum) maṭalil potintu vikka vāruṅkaḷ; makiḻcci illātiruntēmellām makiḻumpaṭi cōtiṭattāl ipparaṇiyi aṟintēṉ eṉṟu kūṟiya kaṇitappēykku akamakiḻntu vāruṅkaḷ; melliya kuṭarākiya niṇatti atakkuṅkaḷ; melliya viralākiya iñciyi melluṅkaḷ; muṉki elumpiṉik kaṭittuk koḷḷuṅkaḷ; mūḷiyi vāri viḻuṅkuṅkaḷ; arintiṭṭa tāmari moṭṭu eṉṉum uḷḷiyik kaṟittuṇmiṉ; ūti alittuṇmiṉ; uṭal ōṭi viyarkka uṇmiṉ; unti mūcceṟintu iḷikkumpaṭi uṇmiṉ; taṉakku nāṉku vāykaḷpaṭittukkoṇṭu emakku oruvāy paṭitta piramaṉ nāṇumpaṭi kaḷippōṭu uṇmiṉ; kōpittuk kotitta kariyiṉ kumpattuk kuḷirnta nīr moṇṭu potutta tuḷiyā loḻukaviṭṭup pucitta vāyip pūcuṅkaḷ; kutiriyiṉ kalaṇiyiyum cevic curuḷiyum paraṭākiya pākkup piḷaviyum paṭṭa kaliṅkariṉ kaṇmaṇiyākiya cuṇṇāmpum kalantu maṭittut tiṉṉuṅkaḷ, tāmpūlattip peritum uṇṭu purikkēṟap peṟṟīr! pākkuc cerukkiya pēykāḷ! pūtattiṉ cirattiṉ mayiri mōntāṟ piḻippīrkaḷ eṉa oṉṟukku oṉṟu kūṟi uṇṭukaḷittaṉa.
ivvāṟu kaḷittu vayiṟu niṟintu ēppamiṭṭup paruttu niṉṟa pēykaḷ kuṉṟu kutippaṉa pōṟkaḷattē niṉṟu kumpiṭṭuk kūttāṭiṉa; kutirikaḷi viṭṭuk kaliṅkarōṭa vicayataraṉatu vīrar kutirikaḷik kavarntami pāṭi maṇṇiyum aḷḷit tūvi niṉṟāṭiṉa. pōr tavirntu kaliṅkar ōṭa vicayataraṉ ēviya taṇṭiṉ iru 1kikaḷiṉ veṟṟiyip pāṭi iru kikaḷum vīci niṉṟāṭiṉa; vaḻutiyar nuḻiyum malik kukikaḷ ivi eṉumpaṭi mata yāṉikaḷiṉ vayiṟukaḷuḷ nuḻintaṉa; uruviya vāḷōṭu uyarkaṇiviṭum kaliṅkar uruḷum vaṭiviteṉa uruṇṭaṉa cila; payantōṭum vīrarkaḷiṉ vaṭivu iteṉa talivirittōṭiṉa cila; muracu, kuṭi, kavari, virutu, koṭu, curinta caṅkiṉ nātam mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu cumantu vantu eppoḻutum aracar tiṟiyiṭum apayaṉ aṭikaḷi aṭiya vāruṅkaḷ eṉṟaṉa cila; ivvāṟāṉa nāṭakaṅkaḷ muṭittapiṉ cūriyakulat tutittavaṉum intiraṉi ottavaṉumākiya kuṟṟamaṟṟavaṉatu pukaḻi iṉip pāṭuvēmeṉṟu ellām toṭaṅkiṉa; iru piṟappāḷar uḷḷiṭṭa uyirkaḷ ellām apayameṉap pukuntu añcātirukkumpaṭi apayamaḷitta apayaṉ tāḷkaḷi vāḻttiṉa. nāṟṟicikaḷilumiruntu vanta aracarkaḷiṉmuṉ ataṭṭi urappuñ cicupālaṉ vita vaciyip poṟuttāṉi vāḻttiṉa. kāvirit tuṟivaṉi vāḻttiṉa porunikku ariyaṉi vāḻttiṉa. kaṅki maṇāḷaṉi vāḻttiṉa.
ēḻu kaliṅkaratu āṇiyām cakkaramum ōrāḻiyiṉkīḻ vanta vaṭa kaliṅkatti ūḻikālamaṭṭum kāttaḷikkum, ulakuyya vantāṉi vāḻttiṉa. piramā paṭittaḷitta puviyi iraṇṭāvatum paṭittuk kāppatum eṉ kaṭaṉeṉṟu kātta karikāl vaḷavaṉi vāḻttiṉa.

vāḻttu
eṅkum kaḷiciṟakka; tarumam eṉṟum eṅkum nili uḷḷa tākuka; tēvariṉ aruḷ ciṟakka; muṉivar ceytavappayaṉ viḷika; vētaneṟi parakka; apayaṉ veṉṟa vevviya kalimaṟika; collappaṭṭa pukaḻ parakkavum puvi nilikkavum maḻi poḻika.
muṟṟum

